[1/17/2023 8:58 PM] taxiivation
I've realized that it is very hard for me to do pistol squats because of a lack of Range of Motion on the lower part of the eccentric. If I try going from the bottom to do a concentric one legged squat, I find that I could do it with my right leg but not my left. I've concluded that my eccentric is significantly weaker than my concentric because of things like skipping the eccentric part of walking down the stairs (kinda like jumping).

I'm going to temporarily switch out the pistol squats in my program to Partial Range of Motion Squats on the lower portion of it to hopefully increase my strength in the eccentric of that range to finally be able to do a full pistol squat.
